- **2026-08-09 (admin approval queue + incremental client collection writes; DEPLOYED)** — Two related fixes deployed together. **(1) Admin onboarding advance — Firestore queue trigger:** replaced the two IAM-blocked callable functions (`adminAdvanceClientOnboarding`, `adminAdvanceCaregiverOnboarding`) with a new `processAdminAdvanceQueue` Firestore `onCreate` trigger on `adminAdvanceQueue/{docId}`. Admin panel writes `{type, uid, createdAt}` to that collection; the trigger resolves the phone from `users/{uid}`, calls `advanceOnboardingStep`, and writes `processedAt`/`error` back. No HTTP endpoint — bypasses the org-level `constraints/iam.allowedPolicyMemberTypes` policy that was blocking new callables. Added 4 override cases to `advanceOnboardingStep`: `admin_identity_override` (advances client past identity gate), `admin_payment_override` (marks membership active, calls `persistClientCareRecords` + `presentPrefilledJobPost`), `admin_caregiver_membership_override`, `admin_caregiver_bgcheck_override`. Added `firestore.rules` grant `allow create: if isAdmin()` for `adminAdvanceQueue` (was the root cause of silent write failures). Files: `functions/src/triggers/adminAdvanceQueue.ts` (new), `functions/src/agents/onboardingConversation.ts`, `functions/src/index.ts`, `components/admin/AdminClientManager.tsx`, `components/admin/AdminCaregiverManager.tsx`, `firestore.rules`. Old callable function stubs deleted with `firebase functions:delete --force`. **(2) Incremental client collection writes:** added a non-fatal incremental mirror in the 'save_onboarding_field` MCP tool handler (`functions/src/mcp/server.ts`). After each field is saved to `agent_sessions/{phone}.onboardingData`, reads `userId` (set at collection-complete) or `webOnboardingUid` (set at session creation for web-OTP path) from the session doc and calls `persistClientCareRecords(uid, phone, data, {})` fire-and-forget. This mirrors each field to `clientIntakes/{uid}`, `carePlans/{uid}`, 'senior_profiles/{uid}`, `job_postings/{uid}` as it's collected — identical to the caregiver doc incremental mirror in `mergeOnboardingData`. Cold-SMS clients (no uid available mid-flow) get the full batch write at `handleClientShowCaregivers` unchanged. Build 370/0, full deploy complete.

New characterization suite in `handleInbound.routing.test.ts` pins that inbound text at every gate-awaiting step routes to the scripted handler and NEVER reaches `runQaAgent`/`runQuickReply`/intent classification (the assumption that makes the scripted-only cooldown sufficient). - **2026-07-10 (permissions-flow trap wave, same day; DEPLOYED-VERIFIED 2026-07-11 ~06:00Z, functions-only)** — Founder screenshot: a fully approved, LIVE caregiver kept getting "Reply YES or NO" texts, the daily stale nudge claimed his profile was "almost there… ready to start picking up shifts", and asking "What's missing in my profile?" got a hallucinated list of missing availability fields (blocked-out dates/locations — fields Evia never collects). Root cause: the post-approval permissions setup (`permissionsConversation.ts`, entered only AFTER bg-check clear + Stripe Connect) owns EVERY inbound text while `onboardingStep` is a permissions step (webhooks.ts routes there before anything else and returns), only exits on a clean YES/NO ×2, had no live-fact grounding, no escape hatch, and no truthful nudge branch — so the session (and ALL of Evia's normal features) stayed blocked indefinitely while nudges misdescribed a live profile as unfinished. FIXES (4): **(1) grounding** — `LIVE_GATE_FACT_BUILDERS` entries for all 5 permission steps (new `buildLiveCaregiverPermissionsFact` / `buildLiveClientPermissionsFact`: "profile/setup is COMPLETE and LIVE, NOTHING is missing, only an optional yes/no question remains"), consulted by `answerPermissionQuestion` (new `liveFact` param), the bailout answer, and (via the existing lookup) the stale nudge. **(2) question-detour bailout** — permissions flow now follows the repo's max-ONE-re-ask confirm-handler rule: `permissionsDetourCount` on the session; the 2nd consecutive question-detour answers the question, defaults every unanswered permission OFF (deny-by-default — never grant what the user didn't say YES to; unconditional grants preserved: caregiver `canShareJournalWithFamily`/`canAcceptJobsWithConfirmation`, client digest/health-alerts), marks the session `complete`+`optedIn`, and unblocks normal routing (shared `finalizePermissionsWithDefaults`; caregiver bailout also emits the `caregiver_onboarding_complete` admin alert with a defaulted-note + job fan-out; client bailout kicks matching via extracted `kickOffClientMatching`). **(3) stale nudge** — 'staleSessionNudge` gained a 7-day auto-complete sweep for permission-stuck sessions (same defaults + a grounded "you're all set" message; runs before the nudge query so completed docs aren't re-nudged) AND dedicated caregiver/client permission-step nudge branches that never claim the profile/setup is unfinished. **(4) YES/NO collision** — `notifyNewCaregiverOfJobs` (U10 job fan-out) moved from the stripe_connect completion handler (where its "Interested? Reply YES or NO" raced the permissions "Reply YES or NO" and the router fed the caregiver's YES to the permissions machine, silently dropping the job application) to permissions completion/bailout/sweep — the session is `complete` (normal `pendingJobId` routing) before any job invite goes out. Gates: transpile 299/0; `permissionsConversation.test.ts` 13/13 (new: bailout defaults both roles, mid-flow bailout preserves already-answered perms, fan-out fires on completion); caraGateWalk + handleInbound.routing + onboardingBgcheckReuse 95/95; **full functions suite 193 files, 2098 passed / 8 skipped / 0 failed**. **DEPLOYED-VERIFIED (founder "pre-deploy env diff + functions deploy", 2026-07-11 ~06:00Z): pre-deploy env-VALUE diff byte-clean (70 local = 70 live identical, 0 different, 0 would-be-added, JWT_SECRET identical len 64; 3 live-only = the platform-injected EVENTARC_CLOUD_EVENT_SOURCE/FIREBASE_CONFIG/GCLOUD_PROJECT); detached functions-only deploy exit 0 — "Deploy complete!", tail all-success; post-deploy: v1-linqWebhook ACTIVE updateTime 06:00:35Z + v1-sendStaleSessionNudges ACTIVE 05:55:30Z, linqWebhook 405-on-GET, stripeWebhook 400-on-unsigned-GET, post-deploy env re-diff still byte-clean (73 live, JWT_SECRET SET+identical). Hosting NOT deployed (zero webapp changes).** OPEN: unstick the founder's own session (reply YES/NO twice, or wait for the new 7-day sweep), fresh-number E2E through the permissions steps, commit.
- **2026-07-10 (job-post contract wave, same day; DEPLOYED-VERIFIED 2026-07-11 ~04:31Z — see deploy note at end of this entry)** — Follow-up to the client parity audit: traced the client job-post pipeline (Evia's post-payment prefilled job-post prompt → `buildAndSaveJobPost` → caregiver Job Board + SMS notify) and found the `job_posts` doc shape had drifted from the web JobPost contract in ALL THREE server writers (Evia builder, `jobNotifications.createJobPost` intake trigger, MCP `create_job_post`): `location` written as an OBJECT (**renders as a JSX child → crashes the caregiver Job Board's Available tab for every caregiver while one such job is open**), 'summary` instead of `title` (blank card heading), `hourlyRate` instead of `rate` ("$undefined/hr"), no `date` mirror, no top-level lat/lng (no distance), no `clientName`, and Timestamp `createdAt` — which ALSO made Evia jobs invisible to the daily 'sendJobMatchNotifications` sweep (string-compared `createdAt >= isoString` never matches a Timestamp). FIX: new shared `functions/src/agents/jobPostContract.ts` (`buildWebJobPostDoc`) emits the exact web contract (title, location STRING, rate+rateFlexible, date mirror, top-level lat/lng, requirements mirror, clientName, ISO createdAt, "card"→"credit" + underscore→hyphen enum maps) plus the server extras (schedule obj, summary, hourlyRate, notifiedCount) — all three writers now go through it. TWO MORE bugs killed in the same pass: (1) **duplicate job posts** — the clientIntakes onCreate trigger wrote `job_posts/{uid}` AND `buildAndSaveJobPost` wrote `job_posts/{autoId}` → same family listed twice, caregivers SMS-texted twice (different jobIds defeat the per-job idempotency); Evia's builder now targets `job_posts/{uid}` (merge) so both paths converge on ONE doc; (2) **pre-payment caregiver blast** — with care records now persisted at intake-confirm (earlier today), the intake trigger would have fired the job post + 25-mile caregiver SMS blast BEFORE the family paid; `onIntakeAiMatch` now gates createJobPost+notifyAreaCaregivers on `users.subscriptionActive`/`membershipStatus==='active'` (matching still runs pre-payment for the show-caregivers preview; the job goes public at payment via Evia's prefill confirm). Readers updated to accept both shapes: `notifyAreaCaregivers` + `caregiverJobMatch` coord chains now try top-level lat/lng first (also fixes the pre-existing gap where WEB-posted jobs' top-level `lat` was absent from caregiverJobMatch's `latitude`-only chain). Webapp defense for legacy docs already in prod: exported `normalizeJobPost` in services/api.ts (object-location→string + lat/lng hoist, summary→title, hourlyRate→rate, startDate→date, requirements→careTypes, schedule.days→daysOfWeek) applied at ALL job_posts read sites (getJobPostsByClient, subscribeJobPostsByClient, getOpenJobs, getAllJobs, JobBoard's three direct-collection reads). Gates: transpile 299/0; new jobPostContract suite 6/6; caraGateWalk 22/22, jobMatchScore, triggers suite, routeIntent, mcp 293/293 — all green; tsc 0 errors in touched files; webapp build clean. **DEPLOYED-VERIFIED (founder "Full deploy with functions and hosting", 2026-07-11 ~04:31Z): pre-deploy env-VALUE diff byte-clean (70 local = 70 live values identical, 0 different, 0 would-be-removed, JWT_SECRET identical; 73 live incl. 3 platform-injected); detached full deploy — "Deploy complete!", 180 successful function ops / 0 failures (the 2 grep "failed" hits were the function NAME v1-retryFailedShiftPayments), hosting version finalized + release complete; live probes: linqWebhook 405-on-GET, stripeWebhook 400-on-unsigned-GET, careconnex-d4c8b.web.app 200 serving the NEW build (live index asset hash D8steUQZ == local dist), eviacares.com 301→www 200, v1-linqWebhook post-deploy 73 env vars + JWT_SECRET SET (updateTime 04:31Z). This deploy shipped BOTH same-day waves (job-post contract + client parity below). STILL OPEN: fresh-number client E2E, commit.**

Confirmed both 07-07 diagnosed bugs still live and fixed them, plus two new finds. (1) **Client name never surfaced on webapp** — fixed BOTH sides: `createFirebaseAuthAccount` now backfills Auth `displayName` on the already-exists path (the common `/start` OTP entry creates the Auth user with no displayName, so the fresh-create branch never ran), and `CareConnexContext` falls back to the Firestore profile name when Auth displayName is empty — role-aware (`firstName` only for clients, because `dbService.getUser` merges senior_profiles over the users doc so `profile.name` is the CARE RECIPIENT's name; caregivers use `name`). Payment case now mirrors 'session.stripeCustomerId` (stamped by stripe.ts before `advanceOnboardingStep`) onto `users/{uid}` AND `customers/{uid}`, identical to the caregiver membership case. (4) **MCP `create_senior_profile` never wrote `clientId`** — `list_household_seniors` queries `where("clientId","==",…)`, so tool-created household seniors were invisible; now writes `clientId` + `diagnoses` (onboarding-shape parity). Healthy (verified, no change needed): `resolveSubscriptionUserId` covers client SMS subs (session.userId set at intake-confirm); `customers/{uid}/subscriptions` written by the generic created-handler so the Membership page status works; no client-side subscription gate blocks the dashboard. Also registered `checkr_mcp_sessions` + `in_shift_updates` in RUNTIME_ONLY_COLLECTIONS (pre-existing contract-guard failure from the last two waves). - **2026-07-10** — **In-shift family updates + arrival capture (founder grilling session → build; CODE-COMPLETE, NOT deployed; ships ON behind `IN_SHIFT_UPDATES_ENABLED`).** Closes the gap where a family hears nothing between caregiver ARRIVED and the shift-end summary unless a care-plan task happens to get acked — a quiet visit produced ZERO family touchpoints. New scheduled job 'sendInShiftUpdates` ('scheduled/inShiftUpdate.ts`, cron `*/15`, registered in index.ts): sweeps `appointments` status==in-progress, and on an elapsed-time ladder (no update ≤90-min shifts; first at 60 min after `arrivedAt`; then every 120 min, family-overridable via client-session `inShiftUpdateCadence`; suppressed within 45 min of scheduled end; hard ceiling 6 family msgs/shift) asks the caregiver ONE rotating, care-plan/time-of-day-aware question (med-confirmation folded in). The caregiver's free-text reply (`routeCaregiver.ts handleHourlyUpdate`, dispatched off new session flag `awaitingHourlyUpdate`) is LLM-structured into a wellness snapshot written to the NEW `in_shift_updates` collection (kept separate from `care_journal` so it never trips the shift-end one-per-appt dedup) and relayed to the family PHI-safe, with a warm buffer-framed ack. If the prompt goes unanswered >20 min, the family gets a FACTS-ONLY heartbeat (built from names+arrival time only; prompt forbids asserting wellbeing or saying the caregiver is unresponsive); ≥2 consecutive silences write a low-severity `admin_alerts` doc (internal, non-punitive). **Cap policy:** new `AgentOutput.bypassDailyCap` (caraAgent.ts) — family relay/heartbeat still honor quiet-hours/DND (skip-not-queue) but are exempt from the 3/day proactive cap; weekly family budget isn't invoked so exemption there is automatic; caregiver question uses `canDrop:false` to reach a working caregiver regardless of quiet hours; `in_shift_update`+`in_shift_heartbeat` added to GROUP_SOURCE_AGENTS. **Arrival capture (fixes a real false-alarm hazard found in triggerEngine):** the no-show block previously ran emergency replacement at start+20min with no arrivedAt → family told caregiver "had to cancel" when they simply forgot to text ARRIVED. New pure `triggers/noShowPolicy.ts` (wired into the no-show block; scan lower bound moved 20→8 min): at start+10min send a caregiver arrival-capture ping ('sourceAgent: arrival_capture`, record `arrivalPingSentAt`); run `runEmergencyReplacement` only if the ping is unanswered 15 min (and no inbound since). New appt fields: `inShiftLastPromptAt/PromptCount/FamilyUpdateCount/UnansweredCount/MedsPrompted`, `arrivalPingSentAt`. Ladder + no-show logic isolated in two PURE modules (`inShiftUpdatePolicy.ts`, `noShowPolicy.ts`) tested at the repo's established pure-decision seam (like `pendingTimesheetNudge`). 2h — client self-care/multi-recipient/location parity verified already in the directive, tests added. **DEFERRED to Deploy B/Phase 4** (they replace or remove the scripted rollback net, so they land WITH the deletion, not before it): 2d loop-throw retry+apology, 2e confirm-name→loop rewire, 2f `__RESUME__` loop-world re-ask. **Validation:** `npm --prefix functions run build` clean (293 files, 0 errors); touched unit suites green (frustrationSignals, onboardingContract, onboardingDirective, caregiverOnboardingDirective, caregiverFieldAbsorber, handleInbound.routing, mcp parity, contractSync, qaAgent.onboarding + eval); only failing tests are the 3 known-pre-existing `onboardingReplay` cases (verified present at the pre-change checkpoint by stashing my diff and re-running). **Founder-run next steps (cannot be automated — spend-gated eval, real phone numbers, prod deploy):** (1) `npm run eval:onboarding` w/ `CARA_EVAL_LIVE` (the U8 gate) → record in launch-config-baseline; - **2026-07-07** — **Caregiver signup fixes wave (plan: `docs/plans/caregiver-signup-fixes-2026-07-07-plan.md`). Includes the session-less bg-check fallback (`docs/plans/bgcheck-notify-sessionless-fallback-2026-07-07-plan.md`).** Six fixes from the 2026-07-07 signup audit: **(P0, Fix 1)** Stripe Connect activation was a single point of failure — the `account.updated` Connect webhook matches caregivers by `caregivers.where("stripeAccountId","==",…)`, but during onboarding the account id lived only in the agent session, so the webhook could never match until finalization had already run (chicken-and-egg). `handleCaregiverSendStripeConnect` and 'sendOnboardingLink`'s `caregiver_payouts` branch now mirror `{stripeAccountId, phone}` onto the pre-created `caregivers/{id}` doc (idempotent set+merge, fresh `caregiverId` re-read for the stale-snapshot resend path), so activation no longer depends 100% on the browser hitting `/done`. Double-fire (webhook + `/done` callable) was already deduped — both call `advanceOnboardingStep(phone,"stripe_connect",…)` and the existing `processedWebhookTasks` guard (stripe_connect case marks the task processed FIRST) short-circuits the second; no new guard added, covered by a new test. **(P1, Fix 2)** `resendStuckStep` → `handleCaregiverSendBgcheck` re-POSTed a fresh Checkr `/v1/invitations` on every stale-session nudge, minting a duplicate candidate/invitation and splitting webhook state (pre-created doc tracks only the FIRST candidate). Added a cached-invite reuse guard at the top of `handleCaregiverSendBgcheck` (reuse when `bgcheckInviteUrl` && `caregiverId` set; fresh POST otherwise; `// TODO expiry` for the ~7-day Checkr expiry). **(P1, Fix 3)** `consider`/'suspended`/`disputed` caregiver notifications in `checkr.ts` used `urgency:"standard"` + `canDrop:true` — a caregiver at their daily proactive cap would silently never learn their check needs attention (compliance-adjacent). All three flipped to `urgency:"immediate"` + `canDrop:false`; admin_alerts and the `clear` path untouched. **(M1)** Waitlist dead-end escaped only on literal START OVER/RESTART; added `parseWithClaude` restart-intent classification (exact-match fast path kept) per the no-keyword-intent rule. **(M2)** The three direct Checkr `axios` sites in onboardingConversation now read `CHECKR_KEY || CHECKR_API_KEY` (matches checkr.ts precedence; latent-breakage insurance, no env change). **(M3)** Renamed the post-approval admin alert type `caregiver_pending_review` → `caregiver_onboarding_complete` (no consumers of the old string — grep-verified) + accurate copy/note. **(M4)** Rewrote the stale CLAUDE.md "Caregiver Onboarding → Known follow-up" identity paragraph to describe the now-unified uid-keyed model (identity debt already recorded closed 2026-07-06). M5 (cold-SMS dropout → orphan login) deliberately skipped — needs founder product decision. Gates: `npm --prefix functions run build` clean; new tests for Fix 1 webhook-match + double-fire and Fix 2 invite-reuse; existing suites green (onboardingReplay's 3 pre-existing failures untouched). No firestore.rules or env-var changes. **Adversarial review wave (same day, 2 fresh-context reviewers + verification):** (a) the plan's step guard for `advanceOnboardingStep`'s 'stripe_connect` case was initially missed — without it, Fix 1's webhook matching let an early payout link (agent tool, no step gating) activate a caregiver with a "background check came back clear" text BEFORE Checkr cleared; guard added (only finalizes from `caregiver_awaiting_stripe`/`caregiver_send_stripe_connect`). (b) `processedWebhookTasks` was read-then-arrayUnion — concurrent webhook + /done callable could both pass; atomic claim added via `claimWebhookEvent(STRIPE_EVENTS_COLLECTION, "advance_stripe_connect_{phone}")` (fail-open, settled after arrayUnion). (c) both new stripeAccountId merges wrapped in `guardSideEffect` (dry-run convention). (d) global START OVER (line ~578) and waitlist restart kept a stale `bgcheckInviteUrl` — Fix 2's reuse guard would resend the OLD invitation (old legal name) after a restart; both resets now null it, and fresh POSTs with an existing `caregiverId` re-point `backgroundCheckData.checkrCandidateId` at the new candidate (handleCaregiverSendBgcheck else-branch + sendOnboardingLink bgcheck case) so webhook matching follows the live invitation. (e) tests hardened: direct assertion of the Fix 1 merge write (was uncovered — revert survived the suite), suspended/disputed branches parametrized, webhook `where("stripeAccountId","==",…)` args asserted, step-guard test. Full suite 1999 passed / 3 known onboardingReplay failures. RESOLVED (2026-07-07, plan `docs/plans/bgcheck-notify-sessionless-fallback-2026-07-07-plan.md`, NOT deployed): the session-less caregiver gap is closed. New 'sendBgcheckNoticeToCaregiver` helper in checkr.ts routes all three bad-news branches through the interaction agent when a session exists, else falls back to 'sendToPhone` (creates chat + seeds session, enforces STOP), and pages ops with a high-severity `bgcheck_notice_undelivered` admin_alert on any non-'sent`/`queued` outcome. checkrBadNewsNotify.test.ts grown to 5 tests. - **2026-07-06** — **Confirm-name loop fix: Evia no longer re-asks the name (founder screenshots — caregiver signup asked "do you go by anahi?" 4 times, plus a leaked third-person status line "I'll keep her on track…").** Root cause was two-fold in `agents/onboardingConversation.ts`: (1) the file kept its own CONTEXT-FREE `isQuestionOrOther(text)` — the classifier never saw what question was asked, so direct answers like "yes" and "Anahi is fine" were misread as small talk → free-form LLM reply (hallucination-prone; it even asked its own questions, forking the conversation) + name re-ask, forever, step never advancing; the shared context-aware 'stepHandler.isQuestionOrOther(text, currentQuestion)` existed (the earlier fix) but this file never adopted it — worse, 'stepDeps` passed the local 1-arg copy to `conversationStep`, which calls it WITH the question, silently dropped; (2) both confirm-name handlers ran the question-gate BEFORE parsing the confirmation. Fixes: local copy deleted → shared version imported (all table-driven steps now classify with their re-ask as context); ALL 18 hand-rolled call sites in the file now pass their current question; `handleClientConfirmName`/`handleCaregiverConfirmName` rebuilt around a shared `parseNameConfirmation` (confirmed/corrected/denied/other, parse-FIRST) with a hard no-loop guard — "other"+question answers with at most ONE confirm re-ask (`confirmNameAttempts` in onboardingData), then accepts the seeded name and moves on; "other"+substantive (e.g. the screenshot's "i am looking for home care, i can travel to san jose…") accepts the seeded name and FORWARDS the text to the next step handler so nothing typed is lost; parse failures fall to "other" (old code re-asked on parse failure = another loop). `answerQuestionMidFlow` prompt gains rules 5+6: never ask a question of its own, never speak about the user in third person or narrate progress. Gates: transpile 290/0; onboarding client/caregiver/story/gate-walk/contract/stepHandler suites 91/91 (4 test mocks re-keyed to the shared classifier prompt); full suite 1926 passed with only the 3 known pre-existing onboardingReplay failures. **DEPLOYED 2026-07-06 — targeted `--only functions:v1-linqWebhook`, Deploy complete, function updated 22:29Z (version 187), 69 env vars intact (66 user + 3 Firebase-injected), alive probe 405-on-GET. Live re-test of the signup name step (fresh phone → "yes") still worth a founder pass.**
- **2026-07-06** — **BG-clearance notification + payout-link hardening + rich caregiver-profile links (`/p/{id}` + `v1-caregiverProfileMeta`). DEPLOYED 2026-07-06 (functions + hosting, Deploy complete exit 0; v1-caregiverProfileMeta created with Function URL; live probe: GET /p/{unknown-id} → 200 text/html via the rewrite with fail-open generic OG tags — per-caregiver injection is unit-tested 6/6, first real-preview verification happens with the next texted profile link).** Founder ask: (a) Evia must tell caregivers they're cleared to be on the platform the moment the background check completes and hand them the Stripe payout-setup link; (b) every link texted to clients/caregivers should render as a rich link — caregiver profiles especially. Audit results: the clear→Connect-link flow already existed (checkrWebhook → advanceOnboardingStep("background_check") → handleCaregiverSendStripeConnect) and ALL outbound texting funnels through Linq ('sendSMS` → 'sendToPhone`; Twilio is video-only), where 'splitTextAndUrls`/link-parts already render every URL as a rich preview card on iMessage/RCS — so transport was fine, but three gaps were real: (1) **duplicate Stripe accounts** — `handleCaregiverSendStripeConnect` called `accounts.create` unconditionally, so every re-entry (resendStuckStep 7-day nudge, webhook re-fire) orphaned the previous Express account and could strand a caregiver's completed onboarding; now reuses `onboardingData.stripeAccountId` (same rule as sendOnboardingLink's caregiver_payouts). (2) **renewal clears were silent** — advanceOnboardingStep dedupes on processedWebhookTasks, so a caregiver whose check was re-run/renewed (sendBgCheckRenewalLink promises "I'll text you the moment results come in") got NO text; checkr.ts clear-branch now detects the already-processed case and texts the clearance directly via sendViaInteractionAgent. (3) **generic profile previews** — profile links (`/caregiver/{id}`) previewed as the marketing card because hosting rewrites everything to the static index.html. New `v1-caregiverProfileMeta` HTTP function + hosting rewrite `/p/** → v1-caregiverProfileMeta`: serves the real built index.html (fetched from hosting, 5-min in-memory cache) with title/og:*/twitter:* swapped for the caregiver's name, photo, rate, city, specialties (HTML-escaped; replacer-function replace to survive `$28/hr` `$`-expansion; respects `profileVisibility: hidden`; fail-open to plain SPA); App.tsx routes `/p/:id` → PublicCaregiverProfile (old `/caregiver/:id` kept for old texts); matchingAgent gallery + permissionsConversation "view your profile" now send `/p/{id}`. Copy: clearance message now explicitly says "You're officially approved to be on Evia, and families can now book you!" (first-name personalized) before the Connect link; post-Connect finalization adds a one-time money note (payouts automatic ~2 business days, text PAYOUT for a free instant payout, balance at /caregiver/payments). Link audit (all verified rich-capable): Checkr invitations, Stripe Checkout/Identity/Connect/billing-portal, Meet links, upload/photo|document, /done task links, profile links — all sent as dedicated Linq link parts or auto-split from text; Stripe/Checkr/Google pages carry their own OG. Gates: new caregiverProfileMeta.test.ts 6/6, onboardingLink + caraGateWalk + client-conversation + webhookIdempotency 31/31, full functions suite 1926 passed (only the 3 known pre-existing onboardingReplay failures), transpile 290/0, frontend build clean. **OPEN: deploy (functions + hosting + firebase.json rewrite) — the /p/ rewrite requires v1-caregiverProfileMeta deployed BEFORE or WITH the hosting release.**

Remaining: live E2E — pay $66.49 → invitation SMS → complete check → report.completed flips caregiver approved; and founder decision on retiring the $24.95 price).** Audit found the background-check pipeline was configured to fail: `CHECKR_PACKAGE` was EMPTY in the live env (onboardingConversation's `??` fallback let the empty string through → Checkr invitations posted `package:""` → 400 → caregivers were texted the meaningless `/done?task=background_check` fallback instead of a real Checkr link; checkr.ts/stripe.ts fell back to `driver_pro`, a slug that doesn't exist on the account), `CHECKR_WEBHOOK_SECRET` was empty (webhook fails closed → any result would be rejected), and **zero webhooks were registered on the Checkr account** — cleared reports could never reach the app. Changes: (1) Stripe (live): product `prod_Upwkd4PG5acRIG` "Caregiver Background Check — Essential Criminal (Annual)" with default yearly price `price_1TqGrEL7Ss5iuUb7gW7DsMtA` **$66.49/yr**, covering Checkr's Essential Criminal cost ($64.49); wired as the caregiver annual price in functions/.env (`STRIPE_CAREGIVER_ANNUAL`/`_PRICE_ID`) and root .env (`VITE_STRIPE_CAREGIVER_ANNUAL`), replacing the $24.95 membership price (old price kept in ALLOWED_PRICE_IDS for in-flight checkouts). (2) Checkr package: `CHECKR_PACKAGE=checkrdirect_essential_criminal` (verified via GET /v1/packages: county_criminal 7yr + global watchlist + national criminal standard + sex offender + ssn trace + motor_vehicle_report — exactly the founder-specified screenings, MVR included in base); all code fallbacks unified to that slug and `??`→`||` so empty env can never post `package:""` again. (3) Link delivery: stripe.ts checkout-completed and annual-renewal paths now capture `invitation_url` (previously discarded — caregiver only ever got Checkr's easy-to-miss email), persist it at `backgroundCheckData.invitationUrl`, cache it on `agent_sessions.bgcheckInviteUrl`, and TEXT it to the caregiver via sendViaInteractionAgent; checkr.ts `initiateCheckrCandidate` stores/returns it; checkrWebhook stores it on invitation.created and on invitation.expired/deleted/cancelled deletes both caches + texts the caregiver to reply for a fresh link (fixes stale-link resends); onboardingConversation caches the real URL in handleCaregiverSendBgcheck/sendBgCheckRenewalLink and no longer pins the /done fallback into `bgcheckInviteUrl`. (4) `CHECKR_WEBHOOK_SECRET` set to the Checkr API key (Checkr signs webhooks HMAC-SHA256 with the API key). - **2026-07-06** — **Double-reply fix: ONE VOICE per agent turn (founder screenshot — Evia texted a canned "no match yet" AND a composed "I'm actively matching" back-to-back).** Root cause: `find_replacement_caregivers` (mcp/server.ts) runs `runMatchingForClient` synchronously, which sent its own status SMS mid-tool-call (matchingAgent.ts no-match escalation) while the QA agent's prompt *instructed* it to send a second status line after invoking — two senders, one turn, contradictory content. Fixes: (1) `runMatchingForClient` gains `MatchRunOptions.suppressConversationalSends` — set ONLY by the MCP tool path; skips the pure-status texts (both no-match messages, the "Which ones would you like to meet?" closer, the catch-path stall copy) while keeping all work (interview_requests, failure counter, admin_alerts, commitment resolution, pendingMatches) and the artifacts only it can send (intro + photo gallery on match). Direct callers (routeIntent ×6, commitmentTracker sweep, caraAgent dispatch) unchanged — they have no agent reply behind them. (2) The tool now returns the REAL outcome instead of blind `{triggered:true}`: `matched` → 'sent:true` (hooks the existing `deliveredToUser` guard — no retry can re-run the search) + `matchesPresented` + instruction to close with ONE line; `no_match` → honest "nothing was texted, your reply is the only message, don't invent names"; `failed` → 'success:false` without `_toolError` (no recovery loop). (3) `request_booking` blocked path now says 'sent:true` + "family ALREADY texted the bg-check explanation — don't re-explain" (second latent double-send). (4) qaAgent prompts: new **ONE VOICE** rule in BOTH client + caregiver prompts (tool results saying 'sent:true`/`instruction` mean never restate), CAREGIVER SEARCH directive rewritten (old text literally caused message #2), exhausted-loop/catch confirm copy now picks link-specific vs generic wording via `deliveredLinkArtifact`. (5) **Shadow-isolation hole closed:** `find_replacement_caregivers` was misclassified in `READ_ONLY_TOOLS` — a shadow run executed it FOR REAL (real SMS + Firestore writes from a zero-side-effect harness). Removed; shadow now synthesizes it. Gates: coverage-smoke +4 outcome-contract tests, shadowIsolation +1 (and reclassification assert), booking blocked-contract asserts; mcp+agents 1266 passed (only the 3 known pre-existing onboardingReplay failures), linq+triggers 194/194, transpile 289/0. **DEPLOYED 2026-07-06 — full `--only functions`, Deploy complete, v1-linqWebhook ACTIVE updated 17:59Z (version 184), 69 env vars verified intact.** NOTE: the visible symptom (repeat no-match escalations in 95050) persists until SCC caregiver supply is seeded — matching excludes seeded/demo caregivers by design.
- **2026-07-06** — **Outbound iMessage tapbacks: `react_to_message` tool (Linq reactions feature complete).** The inbound half of Linq reactions was already live (reaction.added → YES/NO task confirmation + audit, reaction.removed → audit, sticker-only inbound → warm ack), and `addReaction`/`removeReaction` existed in `linq/client.ts` but had zero callers — Evia could receive reactions but never send one. Built the outbound half: (1) `handleInboundInner` (webhooks.ts) now stamps `lastInboundMessageId` on the agent session for every `message.received` (tolerant id chain: `data.id ?? data.message_id ?? data.message.id`; new optional `AgentSession.lastInboundMessageId`), so the tool always targets the user's most recent message without the model knowing ids; (2) new MCP tool `react_to_message` (mcp/server.ts) — all six tapbacks (love/like/dislike/laugh/emphasize/question) + `custom` with `customEmoji`, per Linq `POST /messages/{id}/reactions`; gated to 'session.service === "iMessage"` with a soft `express_in_text` fallback (never a tool error) on SMS/RCS, missing target, or Linq failure — a reaction is a nicety, so failures must not trip the recovery loop; successful reactions audit-logged to `agent_reactions` with `direction: "outbound"`; (3) registered as a SHARED tool: `CAREGIVER_TOOL_NAMES` (both surfaces; CLIENT_TOOLS ~125 of the 128 OpenAI cap) and `CORE_TOOL_NAMES` in toolCapabilities.ts (intent-orthogonal — never filtered); (4) documented in BOTH system prompts (client: heart a photo of the senior, thumbs-up a "sounds good", reaction alone can be the whole answer; caregiver: silent thumbs-up ack for "got it"/"on my way"). Deliberate scope cuts: `removeReaction` not exposed as a tool (no use case for Evia un-reacting), `part_index` left at Linq's default (first part). Gates: new `reactToMessage.test.ts` 8/8, all mcp suites 393/393 (incl. parity 128-cap + prompt-documentation guards), linq suites 153/153, transpile 289/0. **DEPLOYED 2026-07-06 — full `--only functions`, Deploy complete, v1-linqWebhook verified updated 16:42Z, 69 env keys intact.**

- **2026-07-06** — **Payout overhaul + offline payments (Venmo/Zelle).** Live-Stripe audit found the payout pipeline had never moved money: all Connect accounts sit on Stripe's automatic DAILY schedule (stripeConnect.ts never set manual), so manual standard payouts could never succeed, the app's ledger-based instant payout would draw on an already-swept balance, and Evia's three payout paths (SMS PAYOUT flow, request_instant_payout, request_standard_payout) reimplemented Stripe calls with no idempotency keys, no shiftHours claim, and copy promising a 1.5% fee no path actually charged plus a fictional "Friday cadence". Decisions (founder 2026-07-06): (1) embrace automatic daily payouts as the standard rail; (2) instant payouts are FREE to caregivers — platform absorbs Stripe's instant fee (INSTANT_PAYOUT_FEE_* constants retired in billing/config.ts); (3) clients can pay offline via cash, Venmo, or Zelle. Changes: new `payoutCommon.executeInstantPayout` is the ONLY payout creator (balance-based, readiness-gated, 2-min replay guard, doc-keyed Stripe idempotency key, unified `caregivers/{id}/payouts` record + notification) — app callable, MCP tool, and SMS flow all delegate; `requestStandardPayout` callable + `request_standard_payout` MCP tool DELETED; new `getPayoutBalance` callable feeds the cash-out modal the real instant balance; stripeConnectWebhook now ledgers automatic payouts on payout.paid/failed and stamps settled shiftHours/appointments (payoutStatus/paidOutAt) so earnings displays stay honest; new `billing/paymentMethods.ts` (`normalizePaymentMethod`/`isOfflinePaymentMethod`, offline = cash|venmo|zelle) adopted across shiftHours/routeCaregiver/MCP/paymentMethods callable; `JobPaymentMethod` widened to cash|venmo|zelle|credit with selector + label updates across booking/postJob/dashboard UIs and Evia's onboarding job flow (1–4 options); confirmCashReceived (callable + MCP tool + api.ts) generalized to all offline methods; all fee/Friday copy corrected (earningsHandler, qaAgent tool blurbs, payout pages, InstantPayoutModal rebuilt single-option free). Tests: new payoutCommon.test.ts (8), instantPayoutHandler.test.ts rewritten for delegation, caregiverActionTools payout suite repointed. DEPLOYED 2026-07-06 (functions + hosting; v1-getPayoutBalance created, v1-requestStandardPayout deleted; Stripe Connect webhook endpoint verified → v1-stripeConnectWebhook with account.updated/payout.paid/payout.failed). NOTE: payouts still never exercised live — the launch-blocking live payout E2E remains open (needs one real onboarded caregiver + a live charge swept through transfer → auto-payout → webhook reconciliation).

- **2026-07-06** — **Interview reminder cleanup + reply-cancellation exemptions (closes the 07-05 "stale 1h reminder" follow-up; fixes a worse silent-drop found during it).** Two bugs: (1) declining/cancelling an interview never retired its scheduled 1h reminders — family/caregiver got "your interview is in an hour" for a dead interview; (2) BIGGER: the twin-trigger rule (`runTriggerEngine` + `cancelTriggerIfUserReplied`) cancelled EVERY pending trigger once the user sent ANY message — texting Evia "thanks" silently killed interview reminders, medication reminders, caregiver check-ins, post-interview follow-ups, and health escalations. Fixes in `triggers/triggerEngine.ts`: new `ProactiveTrigger.refId` (links a trigger to the record it serves), `cancelTriggersByRef(refId)` (retires pending, spares fired/cancelled, idempotent), and `isReplyExempt()` — exempts time-critical types (appointment_reminder, medication_reminder) and system-directive messages (caregiver_checkin/interview_followup/health_escalation/issue_escalation*/replacement_task/retry_extend_schedule prefixes) from BOTH reply-cancellation paths; `qa_retry` stays reply-cancellable on purpose (fresh turn supersedes; commitment tracker backstops). Wiring: `interviewLinkTrigger.ts` stamps `refId video_interview_<id>` on reminders and a new agreed→terminal branch cancels them + clears `remindersScheduledAt` (re-agreement reschedules cleanly; covers web declines which never pass through MCP); `interviewAgent.ts` (SMS flow) stamps `refId interview_<id>` on both reminders AND the `interview_followup` trigger; MCP `cancel_interview` calls `cancelTriggersByRef` with the right prefix for whichever collection held the doc (SMS `interviews` docs have no status trigger — this is their only cleanup). Gates: transpile 289/0; new `triggerCancellation.test.ts` (isReplyExempt/cancelTriggersByRef/cancelTriggerIfUserReplied) + interviewLinkTrigger suite grown to 14 (agreed→declined cancels + clears; requested→declined no-op; refId asserted) — 19/19; handleInbound.routing 59/59; interviewLinks 8/8. **DEPLOYED 2026-07-06 — full `--only functions`, Deploy complete, 0 failures.**
- **2026-07-06** — **Caregiver identity unification: re-key migration built (backlog #2; code path was already done 2026-06-10).** Audit confirmed the live code invariant — caregivers doc ID = users doc ID = Auth uid = session.userId/caregiverId = `caregiverId` on child records (uid-keyed writes + in-flow re-key at finalization, onboardingConversation.ts:3194-3205) — and that ONLY pre-cutover random-ID `caregivers` docs violate it. Those docs are invisible to every web surface (all read `caregivers.doc(currentUser.uid)`: CareConnexContext:137, CaregiverCalendarPage:215/262, CaregiverProfile/PayoutPage via updateUser) AND write-locked by firestore.rules (`request.auth.uid == caregiverId` doc-ID check, rules:123/132) — legacy caregivers get an empty dashboard and can't edit their profile after phone-OTP login. New `migrations/rekeyLegacyCaregiverDocs.ts` (`v1-rekeyLegacyCaregiverDocs`, x-admin-secret + ?dryRun=1, exported in index.ts): per legacy doc — resolve/create Auth user by phone (E.164-normalized), copy to `caregivers/{uid}` (fill-missing if a uid-keyed doc already exists; full copy + `uid` field otherwise), copy payouts subcollection, seed `users/{uid}` parity (never flips userType), re-point `caregiverId` legacyId→uid across shifts/job_applications/video_interviews/interview_requests/hire_requests/shift_offers/reviews + agent_sessions (caregiverId AND userId), delete legacy doc LAST (idempotent re-run on crash). Dry-run never creates Auth users (reports `wouldCreateAuth`); phoneless/bad-phone docs reported for support outreach, untouched. Checkr webhook lookup is query-based (`backgroundCheckData.checkrCandidateId`, checkr.ts:203) — re-key-safe, no change needed. Live run deliberately NOT executed: it would create Auth accounts on fake numbers and fill-merge each doc pair into one mashed profile. **Founder decision: keep the demo docs for demos; delete them before the real marketing push** (they render as fake supply in FindCaregivers). - **2026-07-06** — **`match_outcomes` backend writers: matching learning loop finally records data.** Closes the gap open since 2026-07-03 ("match_outcomes has NO backend writer") plus the same-day discovery that the web writer `aiMatchingService.recordOutcome` has zero UI callers — the collection was empty in prod, so `buildFamilyMatchHistory` (matchingAgent.ts) and `getOutcomePatternSummary` (outcomeAnalytics.ts) always ran blind. Design: server-side triggers at the collection choke points instead of per-surface client wiring — web (PostsPage/useJobApplications) and Evia (mcp `respond_to_job_application`, 'submit_interview_feedback`) both mutate the same docs, so triggers cover every surface and client-side `recordOutcome` calls would double-count (left uncalled deliberately). New `functions/src/ai/matchOutcomes.ts` `writeMatchOutcome` (deterministic doc ID `${source}_${refId}`, merge upsert → idempotent on trigger retries and evolving statuses; ISO-string `timestamp` matching the readers' `orderBy("timestamp")`). Wired in `triggers/aiMatchTriggers.ts`: (1) `onHireRequestFeedback` extended — positive statuses (coordinator_approved/caregiver_accepted/booking_created) → `hired`, negative → `rejected`; (2) new `onJobApplicationOutcome` — job_applications status → accepted = `hired`, rejected = `rejected` (non-decision statuses like interview_scheduled ignored); (3) new `onVideoInterviewClientDecline` — video_interviews declined with `declinedBy === 'client'` → `rejected` (caregiver declines are availability, not a family decision — not recorded). Gates: transpile 288/0, new `matchOutcomeTriggers.test.ts` 10/10, contractCollections + jobMatchScore 140/140 (match_outcomes already allowlisted in RUNTIME_ONLY_COLLECTIONS — no contract change). **DEPLOYED 2026-07-06** (targeted `--only functions:…` with `FUNCTIONS_DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT=120`): `v1-onJobApplicationOutcome` + `v1-onVideoInterviewClientDecline` created, `v1-onHireRequestFeedback` updated, 0 failures. With the `match_outcomes` rules block released same day, the learning loop is live end-to-end; first real accept/reject/decline will seed the collection.

- **2026-07-03** — **Agentic-reliability wave: 15 gaps closed across follow-through, task management, learning loops, and solo-founder ops.** Founder-directed follow-up to the commitment tracker ("make Evia the best agent — find what's missing"). A 4-scout audit ranked gaps into 3 tiers; all implemented. **Tier 1 (user-facing):** (1) R8 promise enforcement — `detectPromiseWithoutToolCall` widened ("I'm on it", "I'll text you", …) and now ENFORCED: a final reply promising action with zero tool calls records a `pending_commitments` doc (was telemetry-only); (2) dropped-turn watchdog — `turn_watch/{chatId}` stamped on every inbound (webhooks), cleared by any outbound send ('sendOneMessage`), survivors past 10 min become commitments via 'sweepDroppedTurns` (triggerEngine); (3) hard Linq send failures now dead-letter into `linq_outbound_queue` (new 'send_failed` reason + drain branch that redelivers raw chat sends) instead of being alert-only lost; (4) `clearExpiredSessionStates` sends a resume nudge ("we got interrupted while …") for meaningful flows via `RESUMABLE_FLOW_DESCRIPTIONS`/`describeInterruptedFlow` (sessionState.ts) instead of silent state wipe; (5) durable goals — `ActiveGoal.expiresAt` horizon, matching start sets a 7-day "find and hire" goal, booking confirmation finally calls `clearActiveGoal` (was 0 callers); (6) degraded mode — `observability/systemStatus.ts` ('system_status/current`): set on critical provider failure/budget exhaustion, cleared by next successful turn; QA catch sends ONE honest notice/user/hour (`degradedFailureNotice`), triggerEngine holds non-health proactive sends. **Tier 2 (learning):** (7) frustration loop closed — `frustrationRecoveryAugmenter` (current turn via extras + next turn via 'session.recentFrustration`); (8) per-family match personalization — `buildFamilyMatchHistory` (interview_requests passes + match_outcomes) injected into scoring prompt; (9) learned quiet hours — 'scheduled/inferActiveHours.ts` weekly histogram → `preferences.inferredQuietHours`, honored by `isInDND` only when explicit DND off; (10) unified `get_work_in_progress` MCP tool (commitments + agent_tasks(_active) + todos + pendingMatches, overdue-first) + parity row; (11) `get_pending_tasks` now oldest-first with `ageHours`. **Tier 3 (ops):** (12) 'scheduled/adminAlertAging.ts` daily digest re-pages unresolved alerts (24h high / 72h rest, dedup stamp); (13) 'scheduled/opsAnomalyWatch.ts` hourly failure-spike pager (grouped thresholds, 6h anti-spam, critical alert + founder SMS); (14) fast-path LLM fallback now raises the typed provider alert (10-min throttle) + daily `ops_counters` fallback counter (was console.warn only); (15) spend guardrails — `checkDailyTurnCap` (150/user/day) + `checkGlobalDailyTurnBudget` (20k/day kill-switch → degraded mode) in rateLimit.ts, wired into `handleInbound` after the crisis fast-path, fail-open. New collections registered in `data/contract.ts`: `turn_watch`, 'system_status`, `ops_counters`. New env knobs: `CARA_DAILY_TURN_CAP`, `CARA_GLOBAL_DAILY_TURN_CAP`. New scheduled exports in index.ts: `adminAlertAgingDaily`, `opsAnomalyWatchHourly`, `inferActiveHoursWeekly`. - **2026-07-03** — **Commitment tracker: Evia's follow-up promises are now guaranteed.** Founder-reported bug (iMessage screenshot): Evia said "I hit a snag … I flagged it so it does not get lost" and "I'll start pulling caregiver matches now" and never got back — three promise sites had no mechanism behind them: (1) the snag fallbacks in `qaAgent.ts` (retry-exhausted + catch) and `linq/webhooks.ts` only wrote an `admin_alerts` doc, nothing ever returned to the user; (2) the 30s `qa_retry` trigger is silently cancelled by ANY new inbound (twin-trigger rule in `triggerEngine.ts`), stranding the promised answer; (3) `matchingAgent.ts`'s catch sent "I'll text you top matches within the hour" with nothing scheduled. New `agents/commitmentTracker.ts` + `pending_commitments` collection (registered in `data/contract.ts`, doc ID `{phone}_{kind}` → built-in dedupe, single-field 'sweepAfter` query → no composite index): every promise site records a commitment; a sweep in `runTriggerEngine` (5-min cadence) re-runs the QA turn ('skipSend`, delivers only a real answer) or the matching pass, checks "still owed?" against the conversation via quick-tier YES/NO before re-answering, and after one failed re-attempt sends an honest handoff ("escalated to our care team, a real person will follow up") + `commitment_unfulfilled` admin_alert (severity high). Fulfillment auto-resolves: a successful QA answer to the same question, a delivered match gallery, or the no-match message all close the commitment. `runMatchingForClient` now returns `"matched" | "no_match" | "failed"` (callers unaffected) and raises `matching_run_failed` admin_alerts; if even the commitment write fails, the copy no longer promises. Also fixed pre-existing test-mock drift: `CLIENT_TOOLS` missing from `../mcp/server` mocks (goldenTranscripts + 3 qaAgent suites) broke 35 golden-transcript replays locally. - **2026-07-02** — **Durable retry queue for the two silent-drop outbound paths (`linq_outbound_queue`).** 'safeSend`/'sendToPhone` previously *discarded* messages when the Linq circuit breaker was open (line FLAGGED/CRITICAL) or the per-pair rate limit hit — a cancellation notice or APPROVE prompt sent during a line incident was simply lost, and `toolNotify.trySend` still reported `{sent:true}` (Evia would claim "I let them know" about a message that never went out). Now: blocked sends are parked in new server-only `linq_outbound_queue` (`functions/src/linq/outboundQueue.ts`) and drained by a new every-minute scheduled sweep (`v1-drainLinqOutboundQueue`, 'scheduled/outboundQueueDrain.ts`) — transactional claim (queued→sending, attempts++ at claim so crash-loops still cap out), exponential backoff (1m→10m cap), max 6 attempts then `failed` + high-severity `admin_alerts`, per-target oldest-first ordering (a blocked target's later messages are skipped that pass, never delivered out of order), chat-target messages re-run through 'safeSend` so they are re-supervised at delivery time (a chat doc with no supervise context is failed, never sent unsupervised). **Staleness classes:** every queued doc has an `expiresAt` — default 15 min for unmarked sends (a stale "running late" is worse than none), 6h must-deliver for `toolNotify.trySend` action notifications; expired docs are marked, never delivered late. Rate-limited sends become due at the next 60s window. `SendOptions` gains `durability:'queue'|'drop'` (default queue), `queueTtlMs`, 'source`; 'safeSend`/'sendToPhone` now return `GuardedSendOutcome` ('sent|queued|dropped|skipped_opt_out`) — `trySend` maps these honestly (`queued_for_retry`, `linq_line_unavailable`, `recipient_opted_out`; the opt-out silent-'sent:true` misreport is also fixed) and the qaAgent prompt tells Evia a `queued_for_retry` message will deliver automatically (no manual-retry offer). Enqueue failure degrades to the old drop behavior (fail-open, loud log). New composite index `linq_outbound_queue(status,notBefore)`; server-only firestore.rules block; EXCLUSIONS internal-infra row added. Tests: `linq/outboundQueue.test.ts` (enqueue timing/TTL/fail-open + drain deliver/expire/backoff/order/opt-out/final-fail-alert). Committed `35bcc0d` on `fix/cara-bug-hunt-2026-06-29`. **DEPLOYED 2026-07-02:** rules + indexes released, then full functions deploy (`FUNCTIONS_DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT=120`, no `--force` so nodejs20 orphans untouched) — 162 updates + `v1-drainLinqOutboundQueue` created, 0 failures; new function verified live via `functions:list` (scheduled, nodejs22).

- **2026-06-16** â€” **Fixed the weekly caregiver earnings digest, which had been silently broken by the 'shiftHours` rail migration.** 'scheduled/weeklyDigest.ts runWeeklyDigests` still read the legacy `visit_payments` collection ('status in ["pending","paid"]`) for its per-caregiver earnings summary â€” but nothing writes those statuses anymore now that `createVisitPayment` is gone, so **every SMS-completed visit dropped out of the digest** (caregivers would see $0 / no summary). This was the unaddressed "digest readers should migrate" follow-up from the rail-consolidation entry below; no test covered the scheduled job so it stayed green. **Fix:** the earnings loop now reads 'shiftHours` (query by `caregiverId` only â€” no composite index needed â€” then filters in memory). Extracted three exported helpers so the contract is testable: `EARNED_SHIFT_STATUSES` (`approved`/`auto_approved`/`paid` â€” `pending_client_review` excluded as not-yet-confirmed earnings), 'shiftGrossCents` (mirrors `processShiftPayment`'s `grossPay ?? amountCents ?? hours*rate` cross-rail resolution; the prior code summed `amountCents` only, which would have under-counted in-app `grossPay`-only shifts), and `isShiftEarnedSince` (status + 'submittedAt`/`createdAt` window). `runWeeklyDigests` is now exported for testability. **Regression test:** new 'scheduled/__tests__/weeklyDigest.earnings.test.ts` (11 tests) â€” unit-tests the helpers and drives `runWeeklyDigests` through a filter-aware Firestore mock that **throws if `visit_payments` is ever read**, proving earnings come from 'shiftHours`, that only client-approved shifts from the last 7 days are summed across both pay rails ($120 grossPay + $80 amountCents = $200 / 2 visits), and that unapproved/old/other-caregiver shifts are excluded. Gates: functions `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; new suite 11/11; full functions suite 744/744 (60 files) green. NOT yet deployed (deploy note: redeploy `v1-sendWeeklyDigests`).
- **2026-06-15** â€” **Closed the `default_payment_method` end-to-end gap that would have starved the new 'shiftHours` charge rail + pinned the SMS-billing contract with a regression test.** Follow-up to the payment-rail consolidation below. Traced the full off-session charge dependency: `processShiftPayment` ('shiftHours.ts:836`) charges the client by reading `customer.invoice_settings.default_payment_method` (or legacy `default_source`), but **neither client checkout path actually sets that field** â€” subscription-mode Checkout (the primary client path, `onboardingConversation.ts handleClientSendPayment`) only sets 'subscription.default_payment_method`, and setup-mode Checkout sets nothing customer-level (confirmed against Stripe docs). Our `payment_method.attached` webhook ('stripe.ts handlePaymentMethodAttached`) only retried booking tasks; it never set a customer default. Net effect: a client who successfully added a card would still hit "Client has no default payment method" on every shift â†’ `payment_failed` â†’ family nagged to re-add a card they already had. **Fix:** `handlePaymentMethodAttached` now, before the booking-task early-return, sets the just-attached PM as `invoice_settings.default_payment_method` when none is set yet â€” idempotent (never overwrites an existing default), guards `customer.deleted`, fires for ALL attaches (covers the plain subscription-onboarding client with no pending booking task). This webhook fires in both subscription- and setup-mode checkout, so it's the single deterministic point that guarantees what the charge engine reads. **Regression test:** new `linq/__tests__/handleCareNotes.billing.test.ts` (3 tests) pins the SMS-native contract â€” an SMS-completed visit (`handleCareNotes`) MUST produce a 'shiftHours` doc in `pending_client_review` that resolves to a positive charge via processShiftPayment's own gross-resolution logic (replicated in-test so it tracks the real engine), MUST text the family an APPROVE prompt + arm `pendingShiftApproval`, and MUST be idempotent on replay (no double-bill / no double-notify). Gates: functions `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; new suite 3/3 + `handleInbound` routing characterization 20/20 green. **This resolves the "verify default_payment_method is reliably set" follow-up flagged in the entry below.** NOT yet deployed (deploy note: redeploy `v1-stripeWebhook` for the attach-handler change).
- **2026-06-15** â€” **Consolidated visit billing onto the single 'shiftHours` payment rail; killed the dead `createVisitPayment` path (real revenue leak).** Code-trace found two parallel visit-payment implementations: (a) the real rail â€” 'shiftHours` doc â†’ client approval â†’ `onShiftHoursApproved` Firestore trigger â†’ `processShiftPayment` charges the client incl. the **1.5% / $0.50-min platform fee** and transfers net pay to the caregiver's Stripe Connect account (idempotency-keyed); and (b) `billing/visitBilling.ts createVisitPayment`, which created a PaymentIntent with `confirm:false` that **was never confirmed/captured** â€” so every visit completed through it charged $0 and took no fee. `createVisitPayment` was wired into the two **SMS-native (Evia) completion paths**: caregiver care-notes completion (`linq/routeCaregiver.ts handleCareNotes`) and client timesheet APPROVE (`agents/timesheetHandler.ts`). Net effect before this change: **SMS-completed visits â€” the canonical UX â€” never billed the family, never paid the caregiver, and took no fee.** Fixes: (1) 'shiftHours.ts processShiftPayment` now resolves gross pay through `grossPay ?? amountCents/100 ?? finalTotalHours*payRate` (the three rails disagreed on field names â€” Evia/MCP docs stored only `amountCents`, so approved Evia shifts were dying on "grossPay not set") and reads the client 'stripeCustomerId` from `customers/{id}` **then falls back to `users/{id}`** (the id is written to both; reading only `customers` was fragile). (2) `mcp/server.ts submit_shift_hours` now also writes `grossPay/basePay/currency/paymentMethod/payRate/submittedTotalHours/autoApproveAt` so new Evia-submitted docs are self-consistent with the charge engine. (3) `handleCareNotes` no longer calls the dead `createVisitPayment`; it creates a `pending_client_review` 'shiftHours` doc (idempotent on appointmentId â€” won't double-bill if hours were already submitted via the MCP tool), sets the client's `pendingShiftApproval` session flag, and texts the family an APPROVE/DISPUTE prompt (routes through `routeClient` â†’ `approveShiftHoursForClient` â†’ real charge). ## Agent-Native Audit â€” U15 re-baseline (2026-06-25)

Closeout of the `feat/cara-100-agent-native` work. The `/ce-agent-native-audit`
skill is intentionally **not** exclusion-aware, so this is a reviewable manual
re-baseline (Open Q1 resolved): raw numbers from an independent audit pass, then
each item that leaves the denominator is traced to a specific `AGENT_NATIVE_EXCLUSIONS.md`
or `context/capability-map.md` row. **An item may only be dropped if a matching
row already exists** â€” items without one stay as genuine gaps. Live tool count:
128 tools in `MCP_TOOLS` (verified `MCP_TOOLS.length` = 128, 2026-06-25; the
earlier "~129" was an off-by-one estimate).

**Verdict (updated 2026-06-25, all punch-list items closed).** Re-baselined to
**100% on all 8 principles**, every denominator removal traceable to an exclusion
row. ONE asterisk remains, and it is honest: `onboarding` was flipped LIVE at
explicit product direction **without** the KTD-6 real-model eval the plan
recommended for the signup path. The score is "100%" on coverage; the residual
RISK (real-model sequencing on signup) is unmeasured, not absent. Mitigations:
the dispatcher only re-sequences (gates untouched), deterministic parity is proven
across the corpus, and `CONVERGENCE_UNFLIPPED=onboarding` is the one-flag rollback.

5. ~~`manage_recurring_schedule`~~ â€” **DONE** resolved as an action-discriminated-primitive exclusion row [e3e0e3f]
6. ~~capability-map reconcile~~ â€” **DONE** missing rows added [e3e0e3f]

**Recommended follow-up (not blocking):** build the KTD-6 real-model eval to
*validate* the onboarding flip after the fact, and watch signup-completion metrics
post-deploy. The flip is reversible if they regress.

## Architecture Decisions

- **2026-06-01** â€” `context/` files are additive to CLAUDE.md (CLAUDE.md stays canonical and links to them) rather than replacing it, to avoid creating duplicate sources of truth.
- **2026-06-01** â€” Vendored `third_party/` is excluded from our typecheck; it carries its own (unmet) dependencies and is not Evia code.
- **2026-05-31** â€” Caregiver search visibility is gated on `onboardingStatus === 'profile_complete'`; both onboarding paths (Evia SMS, web wizard) must set it. This is now an invariant.

## Session Notes

- Type-safety scope was chosen deliberately. Measured fallout: full 'strict` = ~253 Evia errors (187 null-safety); `noImplicitAny` alone = 52. Both would be large diffs with a red type gate, so instead we installed the *enforcement mechanism* green now (excluded `third_party`, fixed the 2 real bugs, added `typecheck` + build gate) and staged the strictness ramp as backlog item #1. A green gate today is worth more than a half-on 'strict` with 50â€“250 failing checks.
- Build pipeline note: `vite build` (esbuild) does NOT typecheck â€” `npm run typecheck` is the real type gate.
